Solved counts are out of the true and false cases, declined correctly out of the contingent ones (a contingent case is never solved: the right response there is no verdict); —: no such case.

Timing

Solved cases: 0.615 s in total, median 0.00906 s per case, over 65 solved case(s) with a time. Times are wall-clock seconds per query as the runner measured them (each query in its own forked process; setup and warm-up not timed); totals and medians are over the solved cases only, so a timeout, error or no verdict never counts as a fast answer. They are shown next to the counts, never combined with them.

Measured on machine pi5 · indicative: it has some other load: times are a fair guide, large differences between its runs mean something, small ones may be noise. Raspberry Pi 5, 4 cores. Benchmark runs go one at a time, in a low-priority systemd unit (CPUWeight=20, Nice=10); since 2026-10-17, each eval is pinned to core 3 (earlier runs were not pinned). The Pi also serves websites, and other work may share core 3 unless it is reserved for the runs (pi5-deploy INSTALL.md, step 15). Clock frequency and temperature are not controlled.

measured setup time: 0.36 s

from sympy import *
import sympy

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content

# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, I, evaluate=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Ne(1 / x, -I, evaluate=False)
Residual = Abs(tanh(asinh(x))) - cos(atan(1 / x))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function

# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number

from sympy import *
import sympy

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

x = Symbol('x', complex=True, imaginary=True, zero=False)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): Or(~Q.zero(x), re(pi) > 0)
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(Pow(x, pi))
Residual = log(x) * pi - log(Pow(x, pi))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differencelog(x) * pi - log(Pow(x, pi))→pi*log(x) - log(x**pi)
autoResidual→pi*log(x) - log(x**pi)
answernull

SympyVersion = sympy.__version__
# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

Residual = Pow(0, asinh(10)) - 0
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
differencePow(0, asinh(10)) - 0→0
autoResidual→0
answertrue

from sympy import *

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content

# warm-up: a fixed generic expression, never case content
W = Symbol('W')
Warmup = sqrt(8) + sin(-W) + exp(log(W)) - Abs(-W)
# every query then runs in its own forked process, starting from this state:
# the expression caches are dropped (imported and lazily loaded modules stay)
sympy.core.cache.clear_cache()

# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cos(Abs(asin(x))))
# dropped (not a unary fact about one symbol): ~Q.zero(cos(asin(x)))
Residual = tan(Abs(asin(x))) - Abs(tan(asin(x)))
# automatic evaluation only: no simplify, refine or other function
Result = Residual
# true iff the result is 0; false only if it is a definite nonzero number
Answer = True if Result == 0 else False if (Result.is_number and Result.is_finite and Result.is_zero is False) else None
print(Result, Answer, sep='\n')
